专用交付见issue，共同使用[交接模板](../templates/issue_handoff.md)和[验收模板](../templates/issue_review.json)。执行者、复核者、方法、实际文件、范围覆盖、失�?未尝试、结论与限制必须明确。模板不能当已验收记录。技术自检可放行相应技术依赖，最终业务采用仍须原规则独立核验；这不新增用户逐卡批准流程�?

38: _RE_MERGER = re.compile(r"同一控制|合并范围")
39: _RE_POLICY = re.compile(r"会计政策")
40: _CAUSE_RE = {ERRATA: _RE_ERRATA, MERGER_RETRO: _RE_MERGER, POLICY_CHANGE: _RE_POLICY}
41: 
42: # 否定：按「就近绑定」处理，不再整句广播。裸「无」也纳入，但绑定半径收紧到 8 字，
43: # 避免「无商誉减值……本期发生差错更正」这类跨主题误绑（误绑方向是待核，不会误放行）。
44: _NEG_TOKENS = ["不适用", "未发生", "未采用", "未涉及", "不存在", "未进行",
45:                "无需", "没有发生", "无前期", "均无", "未", "无"]
46: # 「无重大影响」「无实质性影响」是在说重要性，不是在说没发生，不能当否定
47: _RE_MATERIALITY = re.compile(r"^(重大影响|实质性影响|影响|重大)")
48: _NEG_SHORT = {"未", "无"}
49: _NEG_RADIUS = 14
50: _NEG_RADIUS_SHORT = 8
51: # 金额绑定半径：金额常在「对期初未分配利润影响 X 元」之后，离原因词较远
52: _AMOUNT_RADIUS = 24
53: # 肯定：确认发生 / 已采用 / 已调整
54: # 肯定词：必须是谓词，不能是原因短语本身。
55: # 「追溯重述」「追溯调整」多为小节标题（「追溯重述法的前期会计差错更正事项。」），
56: # 列进来会把跨页截断的标题当成「确认发生」。
57: _POS_TOKENS = ["已调整", "已重述", "已采用", "发生", "存在", "采用",
58:                "调整年初", "调整期初", "调整了"]
59: # 肯定词左侧若出现这些字，说明它其实在否定短语里（未发生 / 没有发生 / 不存在）
60: _POS_SUPPRESS = "未没无不非"
61: 
62: # 金额：负向后视，避免 100.00元 命中 0.00元
63: _RE_ZERO = re.compile(r"(?<![\d.])0\.00\s*元")
64: _RE_AMOUNT = re.compile(r"-?\d[\d,]*\.\d{2}\s*元")
65: # 只对特定项目给出结论（不覆盖本字段）
66: _RE_SCOPED_ITEM = re.compile(r"期初未分配利润|未分配利润|所有者权益|盈余公积|净资产")
67: 
68: SCOPE_LIMITED_METHODS = {"IMPORTANT_MATTER_NA", "NOTE_RETAINED_ERRATA_ZERO"}
69: METHOD_STRENGTH = {
70:     "IMPORTANT_MATTER_NA": "SCOPE_LIMITED",
71:     "NOTE_RETAINED_ERRATA_ZERO": "SCOPE_LIMITED_QUANTIFIED",
72:     "NOTE_TRACE_NA": "BOILERPLATE_CHECKBOX",
73:     "CHECKBOX_NA": "BOILERPLATE_CHECKBOX",
74:     "CHECKBOX_NO": "BOILERPLATE_CHECKBOX",
75:     "SUPP_NEG": "NARRATIVE_NEGATIVE",
76:     "NOTE_TRACE_NOT_OCCURRED": "NARRATIVE_NEGATIVE",
77:     "NOTE_RETAINED_ERRATA_NONZERO": "POSITIVE_NONZERO",
78:     "MANUAL_EVIDENCE": "FIELD_LEVEL_MANUAL",
79:     "ASSUMED_NO_PENDING_REVIEW": "ASSUMPTION",
80: }
81: 
82: SIMPLE_SQ = {"FIN.REV.SQ", "FIN.COST.SQ", "FIN.NPDEDT.SQ",
83:              "FIN.NPP.SQ", "FIN.OCF.SQ"}
84: # 只有「两个累计父输入相减」的派生值才需要父输入兼容性。
85: # Q1 单季 = Q1 累计，只有一个父输入，套两期相减是错的（第七轮 C 项）。
86: CUMULATIVE_DIFF_STATUSES = {"DERIVED_CUMULATIVE_DIFF", "DERIVED_QUARTERLY_SUM_OVERRIDE"}
87: Q1_EQUALS_YTD_STATUS = "DERIVED_Q1_EQUALS_YTD"
88: QUARTERLY_TABLE_STATUS = "EXTRACTED_QUARTERLY_TABLE"
89: 
90: 
91: def is_q1(row_period):
92:     return (row_period or "")[4:] == "0331"
93: 
94: # 分句：全角逗号一律切；半角逗号只在不是千分位时切
95: # （`-160,517.06元` 曾被切成两段，8 条事件确认因此丢失）
96: _CLAUSE_SPLIT = re.compile(r"[；;。，\n]|(?<!\d),(?!\d)")
97: # 勾选式取值：□/√/☑ + 适用/不适用/是/否
98: _RE_CHECKBOX_PAIR = re.compile(r"([□√☑■✔●○])\s*(不适用|适用|是|否)")
99: _CHECKED_MARKS = set("√☑■✔●")
100: _RE_CHECKBOX_VALUE = re.compile(r"^[□√☑■✔●○\s]*(不适用|适用|是|否)\s*$")
101: # 纯标题/标签行：只说明「这一节在讲什么」，本身不断言发生或未发生
102: _RE_LABEL = re.compile(r"^(追溯重述法|未来适用法|追溯调整法|会计政策变更|"
103:                        r"会计估计变更|前期差错更正|前期差错|差错更正)$")
104: 
105: Polarity = namedtuple("Polarity",
106:                       "excluded affirmed scoped mentioned conflict")
107: 
108: 
109: def _is_option_fragment(seg):
110:     """该片段是否只由勾选符号 + 选项标签 + 空白组成（选项组的碎片）。
111: 
112:     第九轮裁决 B 项：原 PDF 里每个 `□` 后插入换行时，选项组被 `_CLAUSE_SPLIT`
113:     切碎成 `□` / `会计政策变更 √` / `会计差错更正 □` …，每片各自还能解出一个
114:     自洽但**反向**的取值（√ 绑到前一片的标签上）。必须先把碎片拼回完整选项组。
115:     """
116:     s = _RE_MARK.sub("", seg or "")
117:     for lab, _c in _OPT_LABELS:
118:         s = s.replace(lab, "")
119:     return re.sub(r"[\s\u3000]", "", s) == ""
120: 
121: 
122: def _is_cause_label(seg):
123:     """纯原因标签（`前期差错更正` / `会计政策变更` …）：勾选组的主语行，不属于组内选项。"""
124:     return bool(_cause_spans(seg or "")) and not _RE_MARK.search(seg or "")
125: 
126: 
127: def _clauses(txt):
128:     """分句，但**不切开选项组**：连续碎片在含 ≥2 个符号时先拼回一组。
129: 
130:     只合并「含 ≥2 个符号」的连续碎片——单符号碎片合并会改变纯标签行的语义
131:     （`会计政策变更` / `会计差错更正` 两行本应各自成立）。
132: 
133:     主语行必须留在组外：`前期差错更正\\n√适用□不适用` 若把标题并进组，符号左侧
134:     也能解出一套自洽取值（√ 认领「前期差错更正」），会与右侧解冲突而假判歧义。
135:     """
136:     raw = [c for c in _CLAUSE_SPLIT.split(txt or "") if c.strip()]
137:     out, buf = [], []
138: 
139:     def flush():
140:         if not buf:
141:             return
142:         head = buf[0]
143:         if len(buf) > 1 and _is_cause_label(head):
144:             out.append(head.strip())
145:             buf[:] = buf[1:]
146:         marks = sum(len(_RE_MARK.findall(x)) for x in buf)
147:         if len(buf) > 1 and marks >= 2:
148:             out.append(" ".join(x.strip() for x in buf))
149:         else:
150:             out.extend(buf)
151:         buf[:] = []
152: 
153:     for c in raw:
154:         if not _is_option_fragment(c):
155:             flush()
156:             out.append(c)
157:             continue
158:         # 组内已出现符号后又来一个纯原因标签：这是下一组的主语，必须先断开
159:         if _is_cause_label(c) and any(_RE_MARK.search(x) for x in buf):
160:             flush()
161:         buf.append(c)
162:     flush()
163:     return out
164: 
165: 
166: def checkbox_value(txt):
167:     """返回被勾选的那一项（适用/不适用/是/否）；无法唯一判定返回 None。
168: 
169:     必须看符号，不能看文字：`□适用√不适用` 与 `√适用□不适用` 都含「不适用」。
170:     """
171:     pairs = _RE_CHECKBOX_PAIR.findall(txt or "")
172:     if not pairs:
173:         return None
174:     checked = [v for m, v in pairs if m in _CHECKED_MARKS]
175:     if len(checked) == 1:
176:         return checked[0]
177:     return None
178: 
179: 
180: def _cause_spans(clause):
181:     spans = []
182:     for cause, rx in _CAUSE_RE.items():
183:         for m in rx.finditer(clause):
184:             spans.append((m.start(), m.end(), cause))
185:     spans.sort()
186:     return spans
187: 
188: 
189: def _nearest(spans, pos, limit=None):
190:     """离 pos 最近的原因（按区间距离）；超出 limit 视为未绑定。"""
191:     best, bestd = None, None
192:     for s, e, cause in spans:
193:         d = 0 if s <= pos < e else min(abs(pos - s), abs(pos - e))
194:         if bestd is None or d < bestd:
195:             best, bestd = cause, d
196:     if limit is not None and bestd is not None and bestd > limit:
197:         return None
198:     return best
199: 
200: 
201: def _neg_positions(clause):
202:     out = []
203:     for tok in _NEG_TOKENS:
204:         for m in re.finditer(re.escape(tok), clause):
205:             out.append((m.start(), m.end(), tok))
206:     return out
207: 
208: 
209: def _mask(clause):
210:     """把专有名词遮成等长占位符，避免「未分配利润」里的「未」被当否定词。
211: 
212:     等长替换保证位置索引与原文一致。
213:     """
214:     out = list(clause)
215:     for m in _RE_SCOPED_ITEM.finditer(clause):
216:         for i in range(m.start(), m.end()):
217:             out[i] = "□"
218:     return "".join(out)
219: 
220: 
221: def _pos_positions(clause, negs=(), spans=()):
222:     """肯定谓词位置。
223: 
224:     除「前一字是否定字」外，还要处理跨词否定：`本期未发生采用追溯重述法的
225:     前期会计差错更正事项` 里的「采用」被「未发生」管辖，不能算肯定——否则与
226:     否定撞成假冲突（第八轮 618 条误冲突的根因）。判据：该肯定之前存在否定词，
227:     且否定词与该肯定之间没有再出现别的原因词（出现则说明否定只管前半句）。
228:     """
229:     out = []
230:     for tok in _POS_TOKENS:
231:         for m in re.finditer(re.escape(tok), clause):
232:             st = m.start()
233:             # 「未发生」「没有发生」「不存在」里的「发生/存在」不算肯定
234:             if st > 0 and clause[st - 1] in _POS_SUPPRESS:
235:                 continue
236:             if any(ne <= st and not any(ns < cs < st for cs, _ce, _c in spans)
237:                    for ns, ne, _t in negs):
238:                 continue
239:             out.append((st, m.end()))
240:     return out
241: 
242: 
243: # 小节标题：`（二）`/`一、`/`2、`/`注1：` 等，用来判「勾选组的主语是否换了」
244: _RE_HEADING = re.compile(r"^(?:[（(][一二三四五六七八九十]+[）)]|"
245:                          r"[一二三四五六七八九十]+、|\d+\s*[、.．]|"
246:                          r"注\s*\d+\s*[、.：:]?)")
247: 
248: 
249: _OPT_LABELS = [
250:     ("不适用", "NA"), ("适用", "YES"), ("是", "YES"), ("否", "NA"),
251:     ("会计政策变更", "POLICY_CHANGE"), ("会计差错更正", "ERRATA"),
252:     ("前期差错更正", "ERRATA"), ("差错更正", "ERRATA"),
253:     ("其他原因", "OTHER"), ("同一控制下企业合并", "MERGER_RETRO"),
254:     ("合并范围变更", "MERGER_RETRO"), ("未来适用法", "FUTURE"),
255:     ("追溯重述法", "RETRO"),
256: ]
257: _MARK_CHARS = "\u25a1\u221a\u2611\u25a0\u2714\u25cf\u25cb"
258: _RE_MARK = re.compile(r"[%s]" % _MARK_CHARS)
259: CANON = {ERRATA: "ERRATA", MERGER_RETRO: "MERGER_RETRO", POLICY_CHANGE: "POLICY_CHANGE"}
260: 
261: 
262: def _label_at(text, at_start):
263:     """在 text 的开头/结尾匹配一个选项标签，返回 (canon, 长度)。"""
264:     best = None
265:     for lab, canon in _OPT_LABELS:
266:         if at_start:
267:             if text.startswith(lab) and (best is None or len(lab) > best[1]):
268:                 best = (canon, len(lab))
269:         else:
270:             if text.endswith(lab) and (best is None or len(lab) > best[1]):
271:                 best = (canon, len(lab))
272:     return best
273: 
274: 
275: def _solve_layout(clause, marks, layout):
276:     """按单一版面假设解出每个符号的取值。
277: 
278:     layout="after"：符号在标签前（`√适用 □不适用`），取符号右侧的标签；
279:     layout="before"：符号在标签后（`适用√ 不适用□`），取符号左侧的标签。
280:     选中侧无标签时回退到另一侧（混排版面）；两侧都无标签、或解出的标签重复
281:     （同一选项被两个符号认领）时，该版面假设不成立，返回 None。
282:     """
283:     out = []
284:     for m in marks:
285:         before = clause[:m.start()].rstrip()
286:         after = clause[m.end():].lstrip()
287:         primary, fallback = ((after, True), (before, False)) if layout == "after" \
288:             else ((before, False), (after, True))
289:         lab = _label_at(primary[0], at_start=primary[1])
290:         if lab is None:
291:             lab = _label_at(fallback[0], at_start=fallback[1])
292:         if lab is None:
293:             return None
294:         out.append((m.group(0) in _CHECKED_MARKS, lab[0]))
295:     labels = [x[1] for x in out]
296:     if len(set(labels)) != len(labels):
297:         return None
298:     return out
299: 
300: 
301: def option_group(clause):
302:     """解析勾选式选项组，返回 (checked, unchecked, ambiguous)。
303: 
304:     必须按符号位置判实际取值：`□适用√不适用` 与 `√适用□不适用` 文字完全相同。
305:     两种版面都要支持——符号在标签前（常见）与符号在标签后（`适用√不适用□`）。
306: 
307:     第八轮返工：V7 逐符号同时看左右两侧，把「左侧是上一个选项」当成歧义，
308:     于是任何正常选项行（`□会计政策变更 √会计差错更正 □其他原因 □不适用`）
309:     都判成 conflict，三协电机 15 条重述差异被误放行。现改为**版面整体求解**：
310:     两种版面各自解一遍，取能自洽（标签互不重复）的那一种；两种都自洽且结论
311:     不同才算真歧义。
312:     """
313:     marks = list(_RE_MARK.finditer(clause))
314:     if not marks:
315:         return set(), set(), False
316:     sol_after = _solve_layout(clause, marks, "after")
317:     sol_before = _solve_layout(clause, marks, "before")
318:     if sol_after is not None and sol_before is not None and sol_after != sol_before:
319:         return set(), set(), True
320:     chosen = sol_after if sol_after is not None else sol_before
321:     if chosen is None:
322:         return set(), set(), True
323:     checked = {lab for chk, lab in chosen if chk}
324:     unchecked = {lab for chk, lab in chosen if not chk}
325:     return checked, unchecked, False
326: 
327: 
328: def _amounts(c):
329:     """分句内全部金额（按出现位置）。零值与非零值必须分开记，不能整句归一。"""
330:     out = []
331:     for m in _RE_AMOUNT.finditer(c):
332:         try:
333:             v = float(re.sub(r"[,\s元]", "", m.group(0)))
334:         except ValueError:
335:             continue
336:         out.append((m.start(), m.end(), v))
337:     return out
338: 
339: 
340: def _bind_amount(c, spans, prev_causes, scoped, affirmed, excluded, neg_c):
341:     """金额句绑定：优先本句原因，其次承接上一分句的原因（主语延续）。
342: 
343:     第九轮裁决 B 项两处修正：
344: 
345:     1. **逐个金额判定**。原 `nonzero = bool(_RE_AMOUNT.search(c)) and not zero`
346:        让同一分句里的零值把非零值一起压掉——`对期初未分配利润影响0.00元及对
347:        净资产影响100.00元` 因此只剩 scoped、丢失事件肯定。
348:     2. **已有 excluded 不得压掉后续非零金额的肯定**。勾选「√不适用」与金额
349:        「净资产影响100.00元」并存时，必须留下 `conflict` 交回原页待核，
350:        而不是静默按勾选放行（原 `t not in excluded` 条件即此绕过）。
351:     """
352:     amounts = _amounts(c)
353:     if not amounts:
354:         return
355:     has_item = bool(_RE_SCOPED_ITEM.search(c))
356:     for st, _en, v in amounts:
357:         tgt = _nearest(spans, st, limit=_AMOUNT_RADIUS) if spans else None
358:         targets = {tgt} if tgt else (set(prev_causes) if prev_causes else set())
359:         if not targets:
360:             continue
361:         nonzero = abs(v) > 0
362:         for t in targets:
363:             if has_item:
364:                 scoped.add(t)
365:             if nonzero and t not in neg_c:
366:                 affirmed.add(t)
367: 
368: 
369: def analyse(rec):
370:     """按分句 + 选项组 + 就近绑定判极性。
371: 
372:     第七轮、第八轮两次返工的要点：
373:     - 勾选式选项组按符号判取值，支持「符号在前」与「符号在后」两种版面；歧义时记冲突。
374:     - 勾选结果只作用于本选项组，不能广播到整句后跳过其余叙述（三协电机误放行的根因）。
375:     - 「无重大影响」是重要性表述，不是否定。
376:     - 金额句在无原因词时承接上一分句的原因，避免「影响0.00元，对净资产影响100.00元」丢失非零事件。
377:     """
378:     txt = (rec.get("evidence") or "") if isinstance(rec, dict) else str(rec or "")
379:     excluded, affirmed, scoped, mentioned = set(), set(), set(), set()
380:     cls = _clauses(txt)
381:     prev_causes = set()
382: 
383:     for raw in cls:
384:         c = raw.strip()
385:         spans = _cause_spans(c)
386:         causes = {s[2] for s in spans}
387:         if causes:
388:             mentioned |= causes
389: 
390:         # 0) 选项组：符号决定取值，且只作用于本组原因
391:         chk, unchk, ambig = option_group(c)
392:         if chk or unchk or ambig:
393:             # 勾选行常与原因标题分行（`前期差错更正\n√适用□不适用`）：
394:             # 本句无原因词时承接上一分句的主语，不能因「本句没提原因」而丢弃勾选结果。
395:             tgt = causes or prev_causes
396:             if ambig:
397:                 for cs in tgt:
398:                     excluded.add(cs)
399:                     affirmed.add(cs)
400:             else:
401:                 explicit = {CANON[cs] for cs in tgt}
402:                 for cs in tgt:
403:                     lab = CANON[cs]
404:                     if lab in chk:
405:                         affirmed.add(cs)
406:                     elif lab in unchk:
407:                         excluded.add(cs)
408:                 # 组内只有「适用/不适用」而无具体原因标签：作用到本组主语
409:                 if "YES" in chk and not (explicit & chk):
410:                     for cs in tgt:
411:                         if CANON[cs] not in unchk:
412:                             affirmed.add(cs)
413:                 if "NA" in chk:
414:                     for cs in tgt:
415:                         if CANON[cs] not in chk:
416:                             excluded.add(cs)
417:             if causes:
418:                 _bind_amount(c, spans, prev_causes, scoped, affirmed, excluded, set())
419:                 prev_causes = causes
420:             continue
421: 
422:         if not causes:
423:             # 标题行：主语换到别的话题（`（四）审批程序及其他说明`），
424:             # 后续勾选组不得再绑到上一个差错更正标题上。
425:             if _RE_HEADING.match(c):
426:                 prev_causes = set()
427:                 continue
428:             # 主语承接：本句无原因词，但带金额 → 绑到上一分句的原因
429:             if prev_causes:
430:                 _bind_amount(c, [], prev_causes, scoped, affirmed, excluded, set())
431:             continue
432: 
433:         # 纯标签行（小标题）不断言
434:         if _RE_LABEL.match(c):
435:             prev_causes = causes
436:             continue
437: 
438:         scan = _mask(c)
439:         single = len(causes) == 1
440:         only = next(iter(causes))
441:         negs = _neg_positions(scan)
442:         neg_c = set()
443:         for st, en, tok in negs:
444:             if tok in _NEG_SHORT and _RE_MATERIALITY.match(scan[en:en + 6]):
445:                 continue
446:             if single:
447:                 neg_c.add(only)
448:                 continue
449:             radius = _NEG_RADIUS_SHORT if tok in _NEG_SHORT else _NEG_RADIUS
450:             tgt = _nearest(spans, st if st else en, limit=radius)
451:             if tgt:
452:                 neg_c.add(tgt)
453: 
454:         pos_c = set()
455:         for st, en in _pos_positions(scan, negs, spans):
456:             tgt = only if single else _nearest(spans, st, limit=_NEG_RADIUS)
457:             if tgt:
458:                 pos_c.add(tgt)
459: 
460:         if single and neg_c and pos_c:
461:             excluded.add(only)
462:             affirmed.add(only)
463:         else:
464:             excluded |= neg_c
465:             affirmed |= pos_c
466: 
467:         _bind_amount(c, spans, prev_causes, scoped, affirmed, excluded, neg_c)
468:         prev_causes = causes
469: 
470:     conflict = excluded & affirmed
471:     return Polarity(excluded, affirmed, scoped, mentioned, conflict)
472: 
473: 
474: def coverage_of(rec):
475:     """兼容旧接口：只返回明确排除的原因集合。"""
476:     return analyse(rec).excluded
477: 
478: 
479: def polarity_str(p):
480:     return "excluded=%s|affirmed=%s|scoped=%s|conflict=%s" % (
481:         ",".join(sorted(p.excluded)) or "-", ",".join(sorted(p.affirmed)) or "-",
482:         ",".join(sorted(p.scoped)) or "-", ",".join(sorted(p.conflict)) or "-")
483: 
484: 
485: def binds_field(rec):
486:     """证据是否绑定到具体字段/表格/行列（NA 类均为否）。"""
487:     txt = (rec.get("evidence") or "") if isinstance(rec, dict) else str(rec or "")
488:     if (rec.get("method") or "") == "MANUAL_EVIDENCE":
489:         return True
490:     return bool(re.search(r"第\s*\d+\s*[行列表]|附注[一二三四五六七八九十]|\d{3,}\s*元", txt))
491: 
492: 
493: def is_own_period(document_period, row_period):
494:     """显式判断，不再用字符串真值。未知文档期间返回 None。"""
495:     if not document_period:
496:         return None
497:     return document_period == row_period
498: 
499: 
500: def period_scope(document_period, row_period):
501:     """只看「期间」带来的要求。"""
502:     own = is_own_period(document_period, row_period)
503:     if own is None:
504:         return {ERRATA, MERGER_RETRO, COMPARATIVE_SCOPE, DOC_PERIOD_UNKNOWN}
505:     if own is False:
506:         # 比较列 / 分季度表供给别的期间：任何追溯调整都会改写比较数
507:         return {ERRATA, MERGER_RETRO, COMPARATIVE_SCOPE}
508:     return {ERRATA}
509: 
510: 
511: def source_scope(status, row_period=""):
512:     """只看「取值来源」带来的要求。
513: 
514:     第七轮 C 项：Q1 单季等于 Q1 累计，只有一个父输入，不套两期相减，
515:     因此 Q1 不要求 PARENT_COMPATIBILITY（它要证的是「这个唯一父输入未被重述」，
516:     已由 ERRATA 维度覆盖）。
517:     """
518:     if status == QUARTERLY_TABLE_STATUS:
519:         return {QUARTERLY_TABLE_PROVENANCE}
520:     if status in CUMULATIVE_DIFF_STATUSES and not is_q1(row_period):
521:         return {PARENT_COMPATIBILITY}
522:     return set()
523: 
524: 
525: def required_scope(status, document_period, row_period):
526:     """该行要断言「未被重述」，必须覆盖哪些维度。
527: 
528:     第七轮 A 项：期间要求与来源要求**取并集**。V6 在非自身期间分支直接返回，
529:     把 QUARTERLY_TABLE_PROVENANCE / PARENT_COMPATIBILITY 覆盖掉了。
530:     """
531:     return period_scope(document_period, row_period) | source_scope(status, row_period)
532: 
533: 
534: def source_dims_for(status, document_period, row_period):
535:     """只看来源维度的缺口，用于「B=是」的确认类断言。"""
536:     req = required_scope(status, document_period, row_period)
537:     return {d for d in req if d in SOURCE_DIMS or d == DOC_PERIOD_UNKNOWN}
538: 
539: 
540: def judge(rec, doc, status, document_period, row_period, ruled=None, bval="",
541:           parent_ok=None, scope_ok=None):
542:     """返回 dict：resolution / scope_required / scope_covered / scope_missing / evidence_note / polarity。
543: 
544:     `scope_ok`：该行已取得结构化核证记录的来源维度集合（比较范围 / 季度表来源）。
545:     第八轮 C 项：这些维度不能靠「文档里提到过」自动覆盖，只能由核证登记表供给；
546:     未传或为空即视为未核，仍留在 scope_missing。
547:     """
548:     def out(res, req=(), cov=(), mis=(), note="", pol="", lvl=""):
549:         return {"resolution": res,
550:                 "scope_level": lvl,
551:                 "scope_required": ";".join(sorted(req)),
552:                 "scope_covered": ";".join(sorted(cov)),
553:                 "scope_missing": ";".join(sorted(mis)),
554:                 "evidence_note": note, "polarity": pol}
555: 
556:     req = required_scope(status, document_period, row_period)
557: 
558:     if ruled:
559:         # 裁决解除不等于「范围已证成」：必需范围照常登记，覆盖留空。
560:         return out("RESOLVED_BY_RULING", req, (), req, lvl="RULED",
561:                    note="独立复核已裁决解除：%s" % str(ruled.get("basis", ""))[:90])
562:     if not doc:
563:         return out("UNRESOLVED_NO_DOCUMENT", req, (), req, lvl="NO_DOC",
564:                    note="B 该行无 document_id，无法定位供给文档")
565:     if rec is None:
566:         return out("UNRESOLVED_DOC_NEVER_VERIFIED", req, (), req, lvl="NO_VERIFY",
567:                    note="供给文档 %s 不在 1,007 份核验清单内" % doc[:20])
568: 
569:     method = rec.get("method") or ""
570:     p = analyse(rec)
571:     covered = set(p.excluded)
572:     if PARENT_COMPATIBILITY in req and parent_ok is True:
573:         covered.add(PARENT_COMPATIBILITY)
574:     if scope_ok:
575:         # 第九轮裁决 E 项：结构化核证只能供给「比较范围 / 分季度表来源」两类来源
576:         # 维度；PARENT_COMPATIBILITY 必须走 parent_verifications.csv，不能被来源
577:         # 登记顶替（否则 parent_ok=False 的行仍可解除）。
578:         covered |= (set(scope_ok) & SCOPE_REGISTRABLE) & req
579:     missing = req - covered
580:     level = "FIELD" if binds_field(rec) else "SECTION"
581:     pol = polarity_str(p)
582: 
583:     # 同一原因既被肯定又被否定：冲突必须待核，不能按任一侧解除。
584:     # 但只有「本行必须排除的原因」冲突才阻断——POLICY_CHANGE 不是必需维度，
585:     # 它自相矛盾不影响「未被差错更正」这条断言（152 条误阻断即由此而来）。
586:     blocking = p.conflict & (req & set(CAUSES))
587:     if blocking:
588:         return out("UNRESOLVED_POLARITY_CONFLICT", req, covered, missing,
589:                    "证据同时肯定与否定 %s（p.%s），须回读原页判实际勾选/金额"
590:                    % (",".join(sorted(blocking)), rec.get("page")), pol, level)
591: 
592:     if method == "ASSUMED_NO_PENDING_REVIEW":
593:         return out("PENDING_WEAK_ASSUMPTION", req, covered, missing,
594:                    "方法为假设（未查原文）p.%s" % rec.get("page"), pol, level)
595: 
596:     # B=是：断言方向相反，要证的是「确实发生过」
597:     if bval == "是":
598:         # 第七轮 D 项：不再用方法名兜底，事件确认必须来自原文极性
599:         event_ok = ERRATA in p.affirmed
600:         src_gap = source_dims_for(status, document_period, row_period) - covered
601:         if not event_ok:
602:             return out("PENDING_CONFIRMED_UNSUPPORTED", req, covered, missing,
603:                        "B=是 但证据未确认发生过差错更正 p.%s" % rec.get("page"), pol, level)
604:         if src_gap:
605:             return out("PENDING_CONFIRMED_EVENT_FIELD_OPEN", req, covered, src_gap,
606:                        "事件已确认，但来源维度未核：%s（p.%s）"
607:                        % (";".join(sorted(src_gap)), rec.get("page")), pol, level)
608:         return out("RESOLVED_RESTATEMENT_CONFIRMED", req, covered, (),
609:                    "证据确认发生更正且来源维度已覆盖（p.%s）" % rec.get("page"), pol, level)
610: 
611:     if method in SCOPE_LIMITED_METHODS and not binds_field(rec):
612:         return out("PENDING_SCOPE_LIMITED_METHOD", req, covered, missing,
613:                    "方法 %s 仅覆盖「重大会计差错更正」一节，未绑定字段/表格/行列（p.%s）；"
614:                    "已排除=%s，仍缺=%s" % (method, rec.get("page"),
615:                                           ",".join(sorted(p.excluded)) or "无",
616:                                           ";".join(sorted(missing)) or "无"), pol, level)
617: 
618:     # 事件已确认但影响只落在特定权益项目：事件与字段影响分开记
619:     if ERRATA in p.affirmed and ERRATA in p.scoped:
620:         return out("PENDING_EVENT_CONFIRMED_SCOPE_LIMITED", req, covered, missing,
621:                    "证据确认发生差错更正，但只给出权益项目影响金额（p.%s），"
622:                    "不能证明本字段受影响；仍缺=%s"
623:                    % (rec.get("page"), ";".join(sorted(missing)) or "无"), pol, level)
624: 
625:     if ERRATA in p.affirmed:
626:         return out("UNRESOLVED_EVIDENCE_CONTRADICTS", req, covered, missing,
627:                    "证据确认存在差错更正（p.%s），与 B=%s 冲突" % (rec.get("page"), bval or "空"), pol, level)
628: 
629:     if not missing:
630:         return out("RESOLVED_SCOPE_COVERED", req, covered, (),
631:                    "证据覆盖该行所需全部维度；method=%s p.%s scope_level=%s"
632:                    % (method, rec.get("page"), level), pol, level)
633: 
634:     if DOC_PERIOD_UNKNOWN in missing:
635:         label, why = "PENDING_DOC_PERIOD_UNKNOWN", "文档期间未知，无法判定是否自身期间"
636:     elif QUARTERLY_TABLE_PROVENANCE in missing:
637:         label, why = "PENDING_SOURCE_PROVENANCE", "取值来源为分季度表，NA 类证据不涉及该表"
638:     elif COMPARATIVE_SCOPE in missing:
639:         label, why = "PENDING_SOURCE_PROVENANCE", "取值来自比较列，未核比较范围"
640:     elif PARENT_COMPATIBILITY in missing:
641:         label, why = "PENDING_PARENT_COMPATIBILITY", "累计差分父输入未证兼容"
642:     else:
643:         label, why = "PENDING_CAUSE_UNCOVERED", "证据未覆盖 %s" % ";".join(sorted(missing))
644:     return out(label, req, covered, missing,
645:                "%s；method=%s p.%s scope_level=%s" % (why, method, rec.get("page"), level),
646:                pol, level)
647: 
648: 
649: def group_key(method, excluded, affirmed, status, is_own):
650:     return "|".join([method or "NONE",
651:                      "+".join(sorted(excluded)) or "NONE",
652:                      ("AFF:" + "+".join(sorted(affirmed))) if affirmed else "NOAFF",
653:                      status or "NONE",
654:                      "OWN" if is_own else ("COMPARATIVE" if is_own is False else "UNKNOWN")])

FILE parent_verify.py
1: #!/usr/bin/env python
2: # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
3: """父输入兼容性 / 来源维度的唯一依据：核证登记表（生产与自检共用）。
4: 
5: 第八轮裁决 C 项明确否掉两条自动判据：
6: - **同文档不能自动证明兼容**——同一报告可能含调整前后列或混合来源；
7: - **上游「双否」不能直接证明兼容**——三协电机已证明 verdict 可能与原文相反，
8:   它只能作为待核线索。
9: 同时澄清：正常同口径累计数相减，**不要求两期调整额相等**；只有主张
10: 「调整影响相互抵消」时才须证明调整额相等。
11: 
12: 第九轮裁决 C 项又指出：只比对「父终值」不够——登记里把父文档换成无关文档、
13: 把父期间和口径写错、审查人填 `x`、日期留空，仍能返回 VERIFIED。因此本轮把
14: 登记记录绑定到**实际父输入**：
15: 
16: - `p1_doc`/`p2_doc` 必须等于该行两个父键实际采用的 document_id；
17: - `p1_period`/`p2_period` 必须等于实际父期间；
18: - `p1_value`/`p2_value` 必须等于实际父终值，且**引文里必须出现该父数**
19:   （按 unit_scale 折算比对，杜绝「引用通用标题片段」）；
20: - 必须登记取值栏目、调整前后版本，且两父版本一致；
21: - 口径三元组不仅要非空，还要与实际父输入一致，并给出 `scope_basis`。
22: 
23: 第九轮裁决 E 项同时限定：来源登记只能供给 `COMPARATIVE_SCOPE` /
24: `QUARTERLY_TABLE_PROVENANCE`，**不得顶替父输入核证**；且引文必须绑定目标键、
25: 目标期间与该行实际供给文档。
26: """
27: import csv
28: import datetime
29: import re
30: from pathlib import Path
31: 
32: import evidence_scope as es
33: 
34: # ---------------- 父输入核证登记表 ----------------
35: COLS = ["security_id", "field_id", "period_key", "ytd_field",
36:         "p1_period", "p2_period", "p1_doc", "p2_doc",
37:         "p1_value", "p2_value", "p1_page", "p2_page",
38:         "p1_quote", "p2_quote",
39:         "p1_column", "p2_column", "p1_adjust_version", "p2_adjust_version",
40:         "consolidation_scope", "unit_scale", "currency", "scope_basis",
41:         "conclusion", "review_role", "reviewer", "reviewed_at"]
42: 
43: # 调整前后版本：两父必须取自同一版本基础
44: ADJUST_VERSIONS = {"ORIGINAL", "RESTATED", "SAME_VERSION"}
45: REVIEW_ROLES = {"INDEPENDENT_REVIEW", "SELF_CHECK"}
46: REVIEWER_PLACEHOLDERS = {"", "x", "xx", "xxx", "-", "--", "n/a", "na",
47:                          "none", "null", "test", "tbd", "todo", "self", "me"}
48: _AMOUNT_TOKEN = re.compile(r"-?\d[\d,]*(?:\.\d+)?")
49: _MIN_QUOTE = 12
50: _MIN_BASIS = 20
51: 
52: 
53: def _nz(s):
54:     return re.sub(r"\s+", "", s or "")
55: 
56: 
57: def _reviewer_ok(ent):
58:     r = (ent.get("reviewer") or "").strip()
59:     return len(r) >= 2 and r.lower() not in REVIEWER_PLACEHOLDERS
60: 
61: 
62: def _role_ok(ent):
63:     return (ent.get("review_role") or "").strip() in REVIEW_ROLES
64: 
65: 
66: def _date_ok(s):
67:     s = (s or "").strip()
68:     if not re.match(r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$", s):
69:         return False
70:     try:
71:         datetime.date(int(s[:4]), int(s[5:7]), int(s[8:10]))
72:     except ValueError:
73:         return False
74:     return True
75: 
76: 
77: def _num_in_quote(quote, value, unit_scale="1"):
78:     """引文里是否出现该父数本身（按 unit_scale 折算）。
79: 
80:     第九轮 C 项：只比父终值字段，登记可以把引文换成无关文档的通用标题片段。
81:     这里要求引文里真的写出这个数，`DOC-…` 之类的标题片段无法满足。
82:     """
83:     try:
84:         target = float(value)
85:     except (TypeError, ValueError):
86:         return False
87:     try:
88:         mult = float(unit_scale or "1")
89:     except (TypeError, ValueError):
90:         mult = 1.0
91:     if mult <= 0:
92:         mult = 1.0
93:     for m in _AMOUNT_TOKEN.finditer(quote or ""):
94:         raw = m.group(0).replace(",", "")
95:         try:
96:             v = float(raw)
97:         except ValueError:
98:             continue
99:         if abs(v * mult - target) <= max(0.05, abs(target) * 1e-9):
100:             return True
101:     return False
102: 
103: 
104: def load(path):
105:     """返回 {(security_id, field_id, period_key): 记录}。"""
106:     idx = {}
107:     p = Path(path)
108:     if not p.exists():
109:         return idx
110:     with open(p, encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f:
111:         for r in csv.DictReader(f):
112:             idx[(r.get("security_id", ""), r.get("field_id", ""),
113:                  r.get("period_key", ""))] = r
114:     return idx
115: 
116: 
117: # ---------------- 来源维度核证登记表 ----------------
118: SCOPE_COLS = ["security_id", "field_id", "period_key", "dimension",
119:               "target_field", "target_period",
120:               "document_id", "source_page", "evidence_quote",
121:               "conclusion", "review_role", "reviewer", "reviewed_at"]
122: 
123: 
124: def load_scope(path):
125:     """来源维度核证登记表：{(sec, fld, pk): [记录, ...]}。"""
126:     idx = {}
127:     p = Path(path)
128:     if not p.exists():
129:         return idx
130:     with open(p, encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f:
131:         for r in csv.DictReader(f):
132:             idx.setdefault((r.get("security_id", ""), r.get("field_id", ""),
133:                             r.get("period_key", "")), []).append(r)
134:     return idx
135: 
136: 
137: def scope_dims(entries, key, evidence_of, expect_doc=None):
138:     """该行已获结构化核证的来源维度。
139: 
140:     第九轮 E 项四条收紧：
141:     1. 维度只接受 `COMPARATIVE_SCOPE` / `QUARTERLY_TABLE_PROVENANCE`；
142:        `PARENT_COMPATIBILITY` 必须走 `parent_verifications.csv`，不能被顶替。
143:     2. `target_field` / `target_period` 必须等于目标键本身（不能拿别的行作依据）。
144:     3. `document_id` 必须等于该行实际供给文档（不能拿无关文档的通用引文）。
145:     4. 引文 ≥12 字、能在该文档原文中定位，且**含该行期间年份**——通用摘录不算来源核证。
146:     """
147:     ok = set()
148:     for e in entries.get(key, []):
149:         dim = (e.get("dimension") or "").strip()
150:         if dim not in es.SCOPE_REGISTRABLE:
151:             continue
152:         if (e.get("conclusion") or "").strip() != "CONFIRMED":
153:             continue
154:         if not _reviewer_ok(e) or not _role_ok(e) or not _date_ok(e.get("reviewed_at")):
155:             continue
156:         if (e.get("target_field") or "").strip() != key[1]:
157:             continue
158:         if (e.get("target_period") or "").strip() != key[2]:
159:             continue
160:         doc = (e.get("document_id") or "").strip()
161:         if expect_doc is not None and doc != (expect_doc or "").strip():
162:             continue
163:         q = _nz(e.get("evidence_quote"))
164:         if len(q) < _MIN_QUOTE:
165:             continue
166:         if q not in _nz(evidence_of(doc)):
167:             continue
168:         if key[2][:4] not in q:
169:             continue
170:         ok.add(dim)
171:     return ok
172: 
173: 
174: def gap(ent, expect, evidence_of):
175:     """返回该记录不足以证明兼容的原因；None 表示依据充分。
176: 
177:     `expect` = {"p1": {...}, "p2": {...}}，每侧含 doc / period / value /
178:     scope / unit / cur，取自 B 的实际父输入。
179:     `evidence_of(doc_id)` 返回该文档可用原文（可为空字符串）。
180:     """
181:     if ent is None:
182:         return "NO_VERIFICATION_RECORD"
183:     if (ent.get("conclusion") or "").strip() != "COMPATIBLE":
184:         return "VERIFICATION_NOT_COMPATIBLE"
185:     if not _reviewer_ok(ent):
186:         return "VERIFICATION_WITHOUT_REVIEWER"
187:     if not _role_ok(ent):
188:         return "VERIFICATION_WITHOUT_ROLE"
189:     if not _date_ok(ent.get("reviewed_at")):
190:         return "VERIFICATION_WITHOUT_DATE"
191: 
192:     # 绑定实际父输入：文档 / 期间 / 终值
193:     for side in ("p1", "p2"):
194:         exp = expect[side]
195:         if (ent.get("%s_doc" % side) or "").strip() != (exp.get("doc") or "").strip():
196:             return "PARENT_DOC_MISMATCH"
197:         if (ent.get("%s_period" % side) or "").strip() != (exp.get("period") or "").strip():
198:             return "PARENT_PERIOD_MISMATCH"
199:         if (ent.get("%s_value" % side) or "").strip() != (exp.get("value") or "").strip():
200:             return "PARENT_VALUE_MISMATCH"
201:         if not (ent.get("%s_column" % side) or "").strip():
202:             return "PARENT_COLUMN_EMPTY"
203:         ver = (ent.get("%s_adjust_version" % side) or "").strip()
204:         if ver not in ADJUST_VERSIONS:
205:             return "PARENT_ADJUST_VERSION_INVALID"
206: 
207:     # 两父必须取自同一调整版本基础
208:     v1 = (ent.get("p1_adjust_version") or "").strip()
209:     v2 = (ent.get("p2_adjust_version") or "").strip()
210:     if v1 != v2:
211:         return "PARENT_ADJUST_VERSION_INCONSISTENT"
212: 
213:     # 口径：非空 + 与实际父输入一致 + 有依据
214:     for f in ("consolidation_scope", "unit_scale", "currency"):
215:         v = (ent.get(f) or "").strip()
216:         if not v:
217:             return "SCOPE_FIELD_EMPTY"
218:         if v != (expect["p1"].get(f) or "").strip():
219:             return "SCOPE_MISMATCH_WITH_PARENT"
220:     if len(_nz(ent.get("scope_basis"))) < _MIN_BASIS:
221:         return "SCOPE_BASIS_MISSING"
222: 
223:     # 引文：够长、可定位、且含该父数
224:     for side, fq, fd in (("p1", "p1_quote", "p1_doc"), ("p2", "p2_quote", "p2_doc")):
225:         q = _nz(ent.get(fq))
226:         if len(q) < _MIN_QUOTE:
227:             return "PARENT_QUOTE_TOO_SHORT"
228:         src = _nz(evidence_of((ent.get(fd) or "").strip()))
229:         if not src:
230:             return "PARENT_SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE"
231:         if q not in src:
232:             return "PARENT_QUOTE_NOT_LOCATED"
233:         if not _num_in_quote(ent.get(fq), expect[side].get("value"),
234:                              expect[side].get("unit")):
235:             return "PARENT_QUOTE_WITHOUT_PARENT_VALUE"
236:     return None

369: def analyse(rec):
370:     """按分句 + 选项组 + 就近绑定判极性。
371: 
372:     第七轮、第八轮两次返工的要点：
373:     - 勾选式选项组按符号判取值，支持「符号在前」与「符号在后」两种版面；歧义时记冲突。
374:     - 勾选结果只作用于本选项组，不能广播到整句后跳过其余叙述（三协电机误放行的根因）。
375:     - 「无重大影响」是重要性表述，不是否定。
376:     - 金额句在无原因词时承接上一分句的原因，避免「影响0.00元，对净资产影响100.00元」丢失非零事件。
377:     """
378:     txt = (rec.get("evidence") or "") if isinstance(rec, dict) else str(rec or "")
379:     excluded, affirmed, scoped, mentioned = set(), set(), set(), set()
380:     cls = _clauses(txt)
381:     prev_causes = set()
382: 
383:     for raw in cls:
384:         c = raw.strip()
385:         spans = _cause_spans(c)
386:         causes = {s[2] for s in spans}
387:         if causes:
388:             mentioned |= causes
389: 
390:         # 0) 选项组：符号决定取值，且只作用于本组原因
391:         chk, unchk, ambig = option_group(c)
392:         if chk or unchk or ambig:
393:             # 勾选行常与原因标题分行（`前期差错更正\n√适用□不适用`）：
394:             # 本句无原因词时承接上一分句的主语，不能因「本句没提原因」而丢弃勾选结果。
395:             tgt = causes or prev_causes
396:             if ambig:
397:                 for cs in tgt:
398:                     excluded.add(cs)
399:                     affirmed.add(cs)
400:             else:
401:                 explicit = {CANON[cs] for cs in tgt}
402:                 for cs in tgt:
403:                     lab = CANON[cs]
404:                     if lab in chk:
405:                         affirmed.add(cs)
406:                     elif lab in unchk:
407:                         excluded.add(cs)
408:                 # 组内只有「适用/不适用」而无具体原因标签：作用到本组主语
409:                 if "YES" in chk and not (explicit & chk):
410:                     for cs in tgt:
411:                         if CANON[cs] not in unchk:
412:                             affirmed.add(cs)
413:                 if "NA" in chk:
414:                     for cs in tgt:
415:                         if CANON[cs] not in chk:
416:                             excluded.add(cs)
417:             if causes:
418:                 _bind_amount(c, spans, prev_causes, scoped, affirmed, excluded, set())
419:                 prev_causes = causes
420:             continue
421: 
422:         if not causes:
423:             # 标题行：主语换到别的话题（`（四）审批程序及其他说明`），
424:             # 后续勾选组不得再绑到上一个差错更正标题上。
425:             if _RE_HEADING.match(c):
426:                 prev_causes = set()
427:                 continue
428:             # 主语承接：本句无原因词，但带金额 → 绑到上一分句的原因
429:             if prev_causes:
430:                 _bind_amount(c, [], prev_causes, scoped, affirmed, excluded, set())
431:             continue
432: 
433:         # 纯标签行（小标题）不断言
434:         if _RE_LABEL.match(c):
435:             prev_causes = causes
436:             continue
437: 
438:         scan = _mask(c)
439:         single = len(causes) == 1
440:         only = next(iter(causes))
441:         negs = _neg_positions(scan)
442:         neg_c = set()
443:         for st, en, tok in negs:
444:             if tok in _NEG_SHORT and _RE_MATERIALITY.match(scan[en:en + 6]):
445:                 continue
446:             if single:
447:                 neg_c.add(only)
448:                 continue
449:             radius = _NEG_RADIUS_SHORT if tok in _NEG_SHORT else _NEG_RADIUS
450:             tgt = _nearest(spans, st if st else en, limit=radius)
451:             if tgt:
452:                 neg_c.add(tgt)
453: 
454:         pos_c = set()
455:         for st, en in _pos_positions(scan, negs, spans):
456:             tgt = only if single else _nearest(spans, st, limit=_NEG_RADIUS)
457:             if tgt:
458:                 pos_c.add(tgt)
459: 
460:         if single and neg_c and pos_c:
461:             excluded.add(only)
462:             affirmed.add(only)
463:         else:
464:             excluded |= neg_c
465:             affirmed |= pos_c
466: 
467:         _bind_amount(c, spans, prev_causes, scoped, affirmed, excluded, neg_c)
468:         prev_causes = causes
469: 
470:     conflict = excluded & affirmed
471:     return Polarity(excluded, affirmed, scoped, mentioned, conflict)
472: 
473: 
474: def coverage_of(rec):
475:     """兼容旧接口：只返回明确排除的原因集合。"""
476:     return analyse(rec).excluded
477: 
478: 
479: def polarity_str(p):
480:     return "excluded=%s|affirmed=%s|scoped=%s|conflict=%s" % (
481:         ",".join(sorted(p.excluded)) or "-", ",".join(sorted(p.affirmed)) or "-",
482:         ",".join(sorted(p.scoped)) or "-", ",".join(sorted(p.conflict)) or "-")
483: 
484: 
485: def binds_field(rec):
486:     """证据是否绑定到具体字段/表格/行列（NA 类均为否）。"""
487:     txt = (rec.get("evidence") or "") if isinstance(rec, dict) else str(rec or "")
488:     if (rec.get("method") or "") == "MANUAL_EVIDENCE":
489:         return True
490:     return bool(re.search(r"第\s*\d+\s*[行列表]|附注[一二三四五六七八九十]|\d{3,}\s*元", txt))

64: def sha(p):
65:     h = hashlib.sha256()
66:     with open(p, "rb") as f:
67:         for c in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
68:             h.update(c)
69:     return h.hexdigest()

72: def num(x):
73:     try:
74:         return float(x)
75:     except (TypeError, ValueError):
76:         return None
77: 
78: 
79: def match(a, b):
80:     x, y = num(a), num(b)
81:     if x is None or y is None:
82:         return (a or "").strip() == (b or "").strip()
83:     if x == 0 and y == 0:
84:         return True
85:     return abs(x - y) <= max(1e-6, max(abs(x), abs(y)) * 1e-9)

89:     d = {}

92:         for r in csv.DictReader(f):
93:             d[(r["security_id"], r["field_id"], r["period_key"])] = r

124: def ruling_file_ok(ent):
125:     """裁决必须绑定真实裁决文件：路径存在、sha256 与登记一致。
126: 
127:     第七轮 E 项：原先只检查裁决 JSON 里出现过 "verdict" 字样，写任何占位文字都能过。
128:     """
129:     rf = (ent.get("ruling_file") or "").strip()
130:     want = (ent.get("ruling_file_sha256") or "").strip()
131:     if not rf or not want:
132:         return False
133:     p = (ROOT / rf) if not Path(rf).is_absolute() else Path(rf)
134:     if not p.exists():
135:         return False
136:     return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == want
137:

145:     rf = (ent.get("ruling_file") or "").strip()

147:         return None
148:     p = (ROOT / rf) if not Path(rf).is_absolute() else Path(rf)
149:     if not p.exists():
150:         return None
151:     return p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").split("\n")

154: def ruling_content_ok(ent):
155:     """裁决依据不能是占位文字，且必须**确实裁决该键**。
156: 
157:     第九轮裁决 D 项：V8 只核对了「文字与行号」，于是把引文换成裁决文件第 3 行
158:     `建议存档名：codex_ruling_round4.md`（确实逐字存在、行号也对）即可通过。
159:     现在要求引文所在的那一行同时给出三样东西：
160: 
161:     1. `ruling_section`：裁决条目名（如 `Q2 大豪科技改判`）；
162:     2. `ruling_key_pattern`：该键的识别信息，且必须真是本键（证券代码/简称/DOC ID）；
163:     3. `ruling_verdict`：裁决结论用语（如 `接受该具体争议解除`）。
164: 
165:     三者与引文必须落在同一行——「无关引文＋其正确行号」因此不再成立。
166:     """
167:     lines = _ruling_text(ent)
168:     if lines is None:
169:         return False
170:     quote = (ent.get("ruling_quote") or "").strip()
171:     if len(quote) < _RULING_QUOTE_MIN:
172:         return False
173:     try:
174:         ln = int(ent.get("ruling_line") or 0)
175:     except (TypeError, ValueError):
176:         return False
177:     if not (1 <= ln <= len(lines)) or quote not in lines[ln - 1]:
178:         return False
179:     if quote not in "\n".join(lines):
180:         return False
181: 
182:     line = lines[ln - 1]
183:     section = (ent.get("ruling_section") or "").strip()
184:     verdict = (ent.get("ruling_verdict") or "").strip()
185:     pat = (ent.get("ruling_key_pattern") or "").strip()
186:     if len(section) < 4 or len(verdict) < 4 or len(pat) < 4:
187:         return False
188:     if section not in line or verdict not in line or pat not in line:
189:         return False
190: 
191:     # 识别信息必须真是本键：证券代码 / 简称 / 该键文档 ID，不能是通用词
192:     sec = (ent.get("key") or "").split("|")[0]
193:     code = sec.split(":")[-1]
194:     idents = {sec, code, (ent.get("stock_name") or "").strip()}
195:     idents = {x for x in idents if x}
196:     if pat not in idents and not re.match(r"^DOC-[0-9A-F]{16}$", pat):
197:         return False
198: 
199:     idents = [t for t in (ent.get("stock_name"), ent.get("key", "").split("|")[0]) if t]
200:     for m in re.finditer(r"DOC-[0-9A-F]{16}", " ".join(
201:             str(ent.get(k) or "") for k in ("basis", "conflict_explanation"))):
202:         idents.append(m.group(0))
203:     for fld in ("basis", "conflict_explanation"):
204:         txt = (ent.get(fld) or "").strip()
205:         if len(txt) < _MIN_BASIS:
206:             return False
207:         if not any(t and t in txt for t in idents):
208:             return False
209:     return True

219:             "unit": (r.get("unit_scale") or "").strip(),

849: def load_reviews():
850:     """内容审查登记表：真正的审查记录，不是可由公开公式算出的摘要。
851: 
852:     第八轮 E 项：原 `review_ref` 是 sha1(键|字段|终值) 的公开摘要，任何人不做审查
853:     也能算出来填进台账；且摘要遗漏 period_key，同一证券同一字段跨期间会互相顶替。
854:     """
855:     idx = {}
856:     if not P_REVIEWS.exists():
857:         return idx
858:     with open(P_REVIEWS, encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f:
859:         for r in csv.DictReader(f):
860:             k = (r.get("security_id", ""), r.get("field_id", ""),
861:                  r.get("period_key", ""), r.get("meta_field", ""))
862:             idx[k] = r
863:     return idx

867: ALLOWED_CONCLUSIONS = {"CONFIRMED_EXPLAINED", "CONFIRMED_NOT_SUBSTANTIVE"}

869: 
870: def _nz(s):
871:     return re.sub(r"\s+", "", s or "")
872: 
873: 
874: def review_key(r):
875:     return "|".join([r["security_id"], r["field_id"], r["period_key"], r["meta_field"]])

877: 
878: ORIG = Path("D:/StockResearchLab/IndustryInflectionLab/robotics_data/collection/originals")
879: _PDF_CACHE = {}
880: 
881: 
882: def pdf_pages(sec, doc):
883:     """回读原件物理页文本（按 document_id 缓存）。不可用时返回 None。
884: 
885:     第九轮 D 项：C24 原来的定位源是 `results.csv.evidence`——那是上游核验记录里
886:     的摘录，不是原件；拿它自证「引文可定位」等于用同一来源自证。改为回读 PDF。
887:     """
888:     key = (sec, doc)
889:     if key in _PDF_CACHE:
890:         return _PDF_CACHE[key]
891:     pages = None
892:     code = (sec or "").split(":")[-1]
893:     pdf = None
894:     for p in (ORIG / code).rglob(doc + ".pdf"):
895:         pdf = p
896:         break
897:     if pdf is not None:
898:         try:
899:             import pymupdf
900:             d = pymupdf.open(str(pdf))
901:             pages = [_nz(d[i].get_text()) for i in range(d.page_count)]
902:             d.close()
903:         except Exception:
904:             pages = None
905:     _PDF_CACHE[key] = pages
906:     return pages

908: 
909: def _supports(printed, value, unit="1", basis=""):
910:     """原文印刷值是否支持该字段终值。
911: 
912:     数值型：按单位折算比对；文本型：逐字包含。
913:     内部枚举名（如 `LEGACY_SNAPSHOT`）不会出现在 PDF 里，此时必须由
914:     `judgement_basis` 同时写出「原文印刷值」与「内部终值」，把映射讲清楚——
915:     这正是第九轮 D 项要求的「针对该字段的判断依据」。
916:     """
917:     v = (value or "").strip()
918:     p = (printed or "").strip()
919:     if not v or not p:
920:         return False
921:     try:
922:         float(v)
923:         return pv._num_in_quote(p, v, unit)
924:     except (TypeError, ValueError):
925:         pass
926:     if _nz(v) in _nz(p) or _nz(p) in _nz(v):
927:         return True
928:     return _nz(p) in _nz(basis) and _nz(v) in _nz(basis)
929: 
930: 
931: def review_gap(r):
932:     """放行一条补充元数据差异所缺的东西；None 表示审查记录齐备。
933: 
934:     第九轮 D 项在第八轮基础上再补五道：
935:     - `reviewed_at` 必须是真实日期，`review_role` 必须显式标注（自检也要标）；
936:     - `reviewer` 不得是 `x` 之类占位；
937:     - `original_page` + `original_fragment` 必须能在**原件该页**逐字命中
938:       （不再接受 `results.csv.evidence` 自证）；
939:     - `original_value_text` 必须是该字段在原文里印出来的字面值，且出现在片段中；
940:     - `judgement_basis` ≥40 字、点名该字段与终值，并给出「印刷值 → 终值」的映射依据。
941:     """
942:     ent = REVIEWS.get((r["security_id"], r["field_id"], r["period_key"], r["meta_field"]))
943:     if ent is None:
944:         return "RELEASE_WITHOUT_CONTENT_REVIEW"
945:     if (r.get("content_review_ref") or "").strip() != review_key(r):
946:         return "LEDGER_NOT_LINKED_TO_REVIEW"
947:     if (ent.get("value_B") or "").strip() != (r.get("value_B") or "").strip():
948:         return "REVIEW_VALUE_MISMATCH"
949:     if (ent.get("conclusion") or "").strip() not in ALLOWED_CONCLUSIONS:
950:         return "REVIEW_WITHOUT_CONCLUSION"
951:     if not pv._reviewer_ok(ent):
952:         return "REVIEW_WITHOUT_REVIEWER"
953:     if (ent.get("review_role") or "").strip() not in pv.REVIEW_ROLES:
954:         return "REVIEW_ROLE_UNMARKED"
955:     if not pv._date_ok(ent.get("reviewed_at")):
956:         return "REVIEW_WITHOUT_DATE"
957:     b = D10.get((r["security_id"], r["field_id"], r["period_key"])) or {}
958:     doc = (b.get("document_id") or "").strip()
959:     if not doc or (ent.get("document_id") or "").strip() != doc:
960:         return "REVIEW_DOCUMENT_MISMATCH"
961: 
962:     # 针对该字段的判断依据
963:     basis = _nz(ent.get("judgement_basis"))
964:     if len(basis) < 40:
965:         return "REVIEW_BASIS_TOO_SHORT"
966:     if _nz(r["meta_field"]) not in basis or _nz(r.get("value_B")) not in basis:
967:         return "REVIEW_BASIS_NOT_FIELD_SPECIFIC"
968: 
969:     printed = (ent.get("original_value_text") or "").strip()
970:     if not printed:
971:         return "REVIEW_WITHOUT_ORIGINAL_VALUE"
972: 
973:     # 原件定位：必须在 PDF 该页逐字命中
974:     try:
975:         pg = int(ent.get("original_page") or 0)
976:     except (TypeError, ValueError):
977:         pg = 0
978:     if pg <= 0:
979:         return "REVIEW_WITHOUT_ORIGINAL_PAGE"
980:     frag = _nz(ent.get("original_fragment"))
981:     if len(frag) < 20:
982:         return "REVIEW_FRAGMENT_TOO_SHORT"
983:     pages = pdf_pages(r["security_id"], doc)
984:     if pages is None:
985:         return "REVIEW_ORIGINAL_UNAVAILABLE"
986:     if pg > len(pages):
987:         return "REVIEW_ORIGINAL_PAGE_OUT_OF_RANGE"
988:     if frag not in pages[pg - 1]:
989:         return "REVIEW_FRAGMENT_NOT_IN_ORIGINAL_PAGE"
990:     if _nz(printed) not in frag:
991:         return "REVIEW_PRINTED_VALUE_NOT_IN_FRAGMENT"
992: 
993:     # 终值支持：印刷值必须支撑该字段终值
994:     if (ent.get("value_in_quote") or "").strip() != (r.get("value_B") or "").strip():
995:         return "REVIEW_VALUE_NOT_DECLARED"
996:     if not _supports(printed, r.get("value_B"), b.get("unit_scale", "1"),
997:                      ent.get("judgement_basis")):
998:         return "REVIEW_PRINTED_VALUE_NOT_SUPPORT_VALUE"
999: 
1000:     # 引文仍保留（用于台账展示），但已不再作为唯一依据
1001:     q = _nz(ent.get("evidence_quote"))
1002:     if len(q) < 12:
1003:         return "REVIEW_QUOTE_TOO_SHORT"
1004:     return None

1060: def _with_review(row, **over):
1061:     """临时注入一条审查记录，跑一次 extra_leaks，再还原（故障注入用）。"""
1062:     k = (row["security_id"], row["field_id"], row["period_key"], row["meta_field"])
1063:     saved = REVIEWS.get(k)
1064:     ent = {"security_id": row["security_id"], "field_id": row["field_id"],
1065:            "period_key": row["period_key"], "meta_field": row["meta_field"],
1066:            "value_B": row.get("value_B", ""), "document_id": "",

1068:            "review_role": "SELF_CHECK", "reviewer": "injection",
1069:            "reviewed_at": "2026-11-11"}
1070:     ent.update(over)
1071:     REVIEWS[k] = ent
1072:     try:
1073:         got = extra_leaks([row])
1074:     finally:
1075:         if saved is None:
1076:             REVIEWS.pop(k, None)
1077:         else:
1078:             REVIEWS[k] = saved
1079:     return got

1082: def _genuine_review(row):
1083:     """按第九轮 D 项口径构造一条**真实可核**的审查记录（正例对照用）。
1084: 
1085:     只取「数值型终值 + 单位=1 + 原件里确实印着该数」的行：回读 PDF 找到该数所在页，
1086:     截取含该数的 40 字片段作为 `original_fragment`，印刷值即该数本身。
1087:     """
1088:     k = (row["security_id"], row["field_id"], row["period_key"])
1089:     b = D10.get(k) or {}
1090:     doc = (b.get("document_id") or "").strip()
1091:     v = (row.get("value_B") or "").strip()
1092:     if not doc or not re.fullmatch(r"-?\d[\d,]*\.\d{2}", v):
1093:         return None
1094:     if (b.get("unit_scale") or "1").strip() != "1":
1095:         return None
1096:     pages = pdf_pages(row["security_id"], doc)
1097:     if not pages:
1098:         return None
1099:     for pi, t in enumerate(pages):
1100:         if v and v in t:
1101:             i = t.index(v)
1102:             frag = t[max(0, i - 22):i + len(v) + 18]
1103:             if len(frag) < 20:
1104:                 continue
1105:             return {"security_id": row["security_id"], "field_id": row["field_id"],
1106:                     "period_key": row["period_key"], "meta_field": row["meta_field"],
1107:                     "value_B": v, "document_id": doc, "source_page": str(pi + 1),
1108:                     "evidence_quote": frag[:20], "original_page": str(pi + 1),
1109:                     "original_fragment": frag, "original_value_text": v,
1110:                     "value_in_quote": v,
1111:                     "judgement_basis": ("核对 %s 字段终值 %s：原件 p.%d 明确印出该数，"
1112:                                         "与 B 的 %s 一致，故判为同一取值，不构成实质差异"
1113:                                         % (row["meta_field"], v, pi + 1, v)),
1114:                     "conclusion": "CONFIRMED_EXPLAINED",
1115:                     "review_role": "SELF_CHECK", "reviewer": "self-check-agent",
1116:                     "reviewed_at": "2026-11-12"}
1117:     return None
1118: 
1119:

1121: for _r in extra:
1122:     if _r["meta_field"] not in ("value_raw", "value_before_adoption", "published_on",
1123:                                 "source_page", "unit_raw"):

    ("前期差错更正\n√适用□不适用，对期初未分配利润影响0.00元，对净资产影响100.00元",
     {"ERRATA"}, set(), set(), "⑧勾选肯定与非零金额并存，一致不冲突"),

def check_all(analyser=None):
    """返回未通过的反例清单；空列表表示全部通过。"""
    import evidence_scope as _es
    an = analyser or _es.analyse
    bad = []
    for txt, exp_a, exp_e, exp_c, why in CASES:
        p = an({"evidence": txt, "method": "T"})
        if (p.affirmed, p.excluded, p.conflict) != (exp_a, exp_e, exp_c):
            bad.append({"case": why, "text": txt,
                        "got": _es.polarity_str(p),
                        "want": "affirmed=%s|excluded=%s|conflict=%s" % (
                            ",".join(sorted(exp_a)) or "-",
                            ",".join(sorted(exp_e)) or "-",
                            ",".join(sorted(exp_c)) or "-")})
    return bad

---
name: "pdf"
description: "Read, create, inspect, render, and verify PDF files where visual layout matters, including fillable AcroForms. Use Poppler rendering plus Python tools such as reportlab, pdfplumber, and pypdf for generation and extraction."
---

# PDF Skill

## When To Use

- Read or review PDF content where layout and visuals matter.
- Create PDFs programmatically with reliable formatting.
- Fill and validate interactive PDF forms.
- Validate final rendering before delivery.

## Tools + Contract Requirements

Immediately before the first create/edit authoring command, run `mark_artifact_operation_started.mjs` successfully exactly once using the command below. Do not run it for read-only work. For edits, replace `create` with `edit`; adjust the expected count and output format to match the requested outputs.



```bash
node container_tools/mark_artifact_operation_started.mjs --operation-kind create --expected-output-count 1 --output-format pdf
```

## Workflow

1. Prefer visual review: render PDF pages to PNGs and inspect them.
   - Use `pdftoppm` from the bundled runtime or system Poppler when available.
   - If unavailable, install Poppler or ask the user to review the output locally.
2. Use `reportlab` to generate PDFs when creating new documents.
3. Use `pdfplumber` or `pypdf` for text extraction and quick checks; do not rely on text extraction for layout fidelity.
4. After each meaningful update, re-render pages and verify alignment, spacing, and legibility.

## Fill And Validate AcroForms

Visual review alone is not a correctness check for a fillable PDF. A page `/Widget` annotation can render a value from its appearance stream while the canonical `/AcroForm/Fields` tree is missing or contains a stale value.

1. Keep the result interactive by default; set `flatten=True` only when the user explicitly requests a completed, static form. Preserve the source PDF, and do not flatten a signed PDF without an explicit workflow decision.
2. Inspect both representations before filling: enumerate fields from `reader.get_fields()` and `/Widget` annotations from every page's `/Annots`, following `/Parent` and `/Kids`. If a widget and a canonical field have the same name but are distinct objects with no `/Parent` relationship, do not call `reattach_fields()` blindly: it can create a second top-level field with the same name. Report the ambiguity or produce a static result.
3. Recover genuinely orphaned widgets, fill all pages, and write the result with `pypdf`:

```python
from pypdf import PdfReader, PdfWriter
from pypdf.generic import NameObject

reader = PdfReader(input_pdf)
writer = PdfWriter()
writer.clone_document_from_reader(reader)

# Restores widgets that are missing from /AcroForm/Fields.
writer.reattach_fields()
fields = writer.get_fields() or {}
missing = set(expected_values) - set(fields)
if missing:
    raise ValueError(f"Form fields not found after repair: {sorted(missing)}")

values_to_write = dict(expected_values)
if flatten:
    # Paint every existing value before removing every widget.
    values_to_write = {
        name: field.get("/V", "/Off" if field.get("/FT") == "/Btn" else "")
        for name, field in fields.items()
    }
    values_to_write.update(expected_values)

writer.update_page_form_field_values(
    None, values_to_write, auto_regenerate=False, flatten=flatten
)

if flatten:
    # pypdf's flatten=True paints appearances but does not remove widgets.
    writer.remove_annotations(subtypes="/Widget")
    writer.root_object.pop(NameObject("/AcroForm"), None)

with open(output_pdf, "wb") as stream:
    writer.write(stream)
```

4. Reopen the written PDF before delivery. For an interactive result, require every expected field to be present in `get_fields()` with the expected `/V`, enumerate page widgets again, and confirm their effective `/V` (the widget value or inherited `/Parent` value) agrees. Confirm each updated widget has a non-empty `/AP` `/N` appearance and render the final pages to catch stale or clipped appearances. Do not rely on `/NeedAppearances` or a successful PNG render as proof that logical field data was updated.
5. For a flattened result, require zero `/Widget` annotations and no remaining `/AcroForm` field tree after reopening, then render the final pages. Keep an editable copy when the user may need to revise the form.

## Temp And Output Conventions

- Use `tmp/pdfs/` for intermediate files; delete them when done.
- Write final artifacts under `output/pdf/` when working in this repo.
- Keep filenames stable and descriptive.

## Dependencies

Prefer the Codex bundled workspace/runtime dependencies when available. The primary runtime is expected to include:

- Python packages: `reportlab`, `pdfplumber`, `pypdf`
- Rendering tools: `pdftoppm` and `pdfinfo` from Poppler

If a dependency is missing, install only what is needed.

Python packages:

```bash
uv pip install reportlab pdfplumber pypdf
```

If `uv` is unavailable:

```bash
python3 -m pip install reportlab pdfplumber pypdf
```

System tools for rendering:

```bash
# macOS (Homebrew)
brew install poppler

# Ubuntu/Debian
sudo apt-get install -y poppler-utils
```

If installation is not possible in this environment, tell the user which dependency is missing and how to install it locally.

1547: def _fmt_parent(v, unit):

1551:         return "{:,.2f}".format(float(v) / mult)
1552:     except (TypeError, ValueError):

200: QMAP = {"0331": "1231", "0630": "0331", "0930": "0630", "1231": "0930"}

202: 
203: def prev_quarter(pk):
204:     tail = pk[4:]
205:     if tail == "0331":
206:         return str(int(pk[:4]) - 1) + "1231"
207:     return pk[:4] + QMAP[tail]
208: 
209: 
210: def _parent_basis(kp):
211:     """父输入的「口径」：文档 + 期间 + 合并范围 + 单位 + 币种 + 版本 + 终值。"""
212:     r = D10.get(kp)
213:     if not r:
214:         return None
215:     return {"doc": (r.get("document_id") or "").strip(),
216:             "period": kp[2],

249:     if fld not in es.SIMPLE_SQ:

256:     if not b1 or not b2:

363: def locate_amount_in_doc(sec, doc, value):
364:     """按金额在原件中定位父数所在物理页（父输入文档常不在 1,007 份核验清单内）。
365: 
366:     返回 (status, page, fragment, unit_hint)。只认千分位格式化后的完整金额串，
367:     避免把 1,234.56 命中 11,234.56 这类子串误判。`unit_hint` 取该页表头声明的
368:     计量单位字面——第九轮 F 项要求父数须交付单位依据，不能只写 unit_scale。
369:     """

371:         amt = "{:,.2f}".format(float(value))
372:     except (TypeError, ValueError):

392:             return ("LOCATED_AMOUNT(p.%d)" % (pi + 1), pi + 1,
393:                     t[max(0, i - 30):i + len(amt) + 10], hint)
394:     return "AMOUNT_NOT_FOUND", 0, "", ""

**第十轮裁决：退回返工（RETURN_FOR_REWORK）。不放行 S04 第二轮正式验收，不放行 S05。**

本轮只读，未修改仓库、未提交、未操作 GitHub。自检通过可以复现，但新增实测仍发现 C24、C14、C38 绕过，父输入单位折算存在实现错误，部分故障注入因前置错误而通过。

**A．极性：上一轮指定整改通过，新增边界测试不通过。**

复算确认：

- `polarity_cases.py` 的 **22 条全部通过**，包括上一轮三条独立反例。
- 相对上一版实现回放 **1,007 份证据**：分句变化 **0**，极性变化 **0**。
- `meta_diff.csv`、`summary.json` 与上一版逐字节一致。
- 三协电机 15 条冲突登记继续保留；传播结论仍只能限定于已核对的直接引用及已实现依赖范围。

新增问题如下：

| 独立反例 | 实测结果 | 裁定 |
|---|---|---|
| `前期差错更正\n√\n不适用` | ERRATA 同时肯定、排除，形成 conflict | 单符号跨行漏合并，假冲突 |
| `前期差错更正\n√\n适用` | 同上 | 同一缺陷 |
| `前期差错更正□\n√适用□不适用` | 排除 ERRATA，最终 `RESOLVED_SCOPE_COVERED` | 含符号的原因标签被并入组选项，歧义未保留 |
| `未发生前期差错更正，营业收入100.00元` | ERRATA conflict | 无关收入金额被承接为事件肯定 |
| `前期差错更正对期初未分配利润影响经重新核算并经董事会和监事会审核后确认为100.00元` | 无 affirmed，也无 scoped | 超出 24 字半径，明确影响金额丢失 |

修正位置：[evidence_scope.py:122](C:/Users/zhangjianmei/Documents/ChatGPT/机器人企业行业拐点研究/robotics-inflection-research/runs/issues/S00/errata_20261111_v5/evidence_scope.py:122)、147、357、429。

`_AMOUNT_RADIUS` 不能单独承担语义绑定；`prev_causes` 也不能让后续所有金额持续继承原因。须识别项目或主题切换，无法唯一解释的选项组保持待核。

带前置原因标题的 `_RE_HEADING` 测试通过：新标题仍能切断旧主语。但这不足以覆盖没有编号的新主题，如“营业收入”。

**B．父输入：没有自动 YES，但登记仍未构成完整核证。**

当前 **自动 YES=0、`PENDING_PARENT_COMPATIBILITY=1,277`** 可以保留。

存在三个问题：

1. **单位折算键名错误。**  
   `_expect()` 提供 `unit_scale`，`gap()` 却读取 `expect[side].get("unit")`，实际回退到 1 倍。见 [parent_verify.py:233](C:/Users/zhangjianmei/Documents/ChatGPT/机器人企业行业拐点研究/robotics-inflection-research/runs/issues/S00/errata_20261111_v5/parent_verify.py:233)；生产构造位置为 `build_errata_v5.py:224`，自检为 `verify_v5.py:225`。  
   真实原件 `DOC-BA230C5D6B13A29F` 的 **187,875.40 万元＝1,878,754,000 元**，正确引文会被该调用链拒绝。直接调用 helper 时 1／1000／10000 折算有效，**不能据此宣称生产路径有效**。

2. **栏目和版本只验证声明形式。**  
   `p1_column`／`p2_column` 任意非空即可；调整版本只验证枚举及两父相等，没有核实其对应实际原文栏目和调整基础。引文含同一个数字，也不证明引用的是目标指标和目标期间。对应 `parent_verify.py:201`、203、224。

3. **生产引文源仍是上游摘要。**  
   [build_errata_v5.py:261](C:/Users/zhangjianmei/Documents/ChatGPT/机器人企业行业拐点研究/robotics-inflection-research/runs/issues/S00/errata_20261111_v5/build_errata_v5.py:261) 向 `gap()` 提供 `vrec.evidence`，没有按登记的父页码回读原件。真实父数即使在 PDF 中，也可能不在该摘录内。

同数值无关内容配任意栏目，在合成源测试中仍返回 `None`。本轮真实 PDF 检查找到同数重复，但重复处仍属同一指标，**未取得严格的“真实异字段同数”绕过实例，不将其冒报为已完成实测**。上述栏目、版本、页码和单位缺陷已经足以判定不通过。

C37 的 20 环可复现，但阳性仍使用合成原文源；须增加真实原件阳性，以及各单位路径、错误栏目、同数异义负例，并断言具体拒绝原因。

**C．C24：仍可伪造，且数值字段也能绕过。**

正例对照可以复现：

`SH:600100|FIN.REV.YTD|20250630|value_raw`，`DOC-F9454692F6BBE32A`，物理第 7 页，本期营业收入 **5,667,075,612.52**，返回 `None`。

但同一页存在上年收入 **6,303,196,235.21**。我保留真实页码和真实片段，把 `original_value_text` 换成这个上年数，在 `judgement_basis` 自述其映射到本期终值，**仍返回 `None`**。

根因在 [verify_v5.py:909](C:/Users/zhangjianmei/Documents/ChatGPT/机器人企业行业拐点研究/robotics-inflection-research/runs/issues/S00/errata_20261111_v5/verify_v5.py:909)：`float(v)` 无法解析带千分位逗号的终值，落入文本分支，再由自述同时包含两个值过关。

枚举绕过也复现：

- 键：`SH:600444|FIN.COST.YTD|20251231|period_source`
- 引文：真实年报封面
- `original_value_text=2025`
- 依据自述将其映射为 `LEGACY_SNAPSHOT`
- 结果：**`review_gap() is None`**

因此，`leak=0`、1,505 格全部待核属实，但目前没有登记，并不能验证未来登记放行安全。须修复数值解析，并按字段规定可核验的映射依据，不能让自述承担全部举证责任。

**D．C14：仍可绕过，大豪科技既有裁决继续保留。**

沿用大豪科技记录，将三项都填为“大豪科技”：

```text
ruling_section = 大豪科技
ruling_key_pattern = 大豪科技
ruling_verdict = 大豪科技
```

引用裁决文件第 **88 行**的文档登记说明：

`ruling_content_ok()` 返回 **True**。该行没有裁决任何争议。

[verify_v5.py:182](C:/Users/zhangjianmei/Documents/ChatGPT/机器人企业行业拐点研究/robotics-inflection-research/runs/issues/S00/errata_20261111_v5/verify_v5.py:182) 仍只检查三个自填字符串出现；第 196 行也允许符合格式的 DOC ID，却没有证明它属于本键。

须绑定已核定的裁决条目及其明确适用键，不能由登记者任意定义“裁决结论”。这不撤销大豪科技原裁决。

**E．C38：白名单通过，证据关系不通过。**

两处 `SCOPE_REGISTRABLE` 均已生效。实测 `parent_ok=False` 且仅提供 `{PARENT_COMPATIBILITY}`，累计差分仍停在 `PENDING_PARENT_COMPATIBILITY`。47 条来源待核未动。

但对真实键：

`BJ:920022|FIN.NPDEDT.SQ|20250630`，实际供给文档 `DOC-EFCBFB68E83B84D0`

使用该文档真实标题片段：

> `Ltd.年度报告——2025——`

即可取得 **`COMPARATIVE_SCOPE` 和 `QUARTERLY_TABLE_PROVENANCE` 两个维度**。它没有证明比较列或季度表来源。

问题在 [parent_verify.py:137](C:/Users/zhangjianmei/Documents/ChatGPT/机器人企业行业拐点研究/robotics-inflection-research/runs/issues/S00/errata_20261111_v5/parent_verify.py:137)：目标字段、期间只是登记值相等；引文仅要求全文命中和含年份，`source_page` 未被核对。

**第 4 步批量核证粒度补充裁定**

1. **9,954 条 `NOTE_TRACE_NA`／631 份文档**：继续允许按“文档版本＋具体栏目＋实际选项组＋覆盖期间”核共同事实，再显式映射成员键。**已解除的 8,655 条／630 份也必须复审**；不能按文档或方法名广播解除。
2. **642 条零值注记／42 份文档**：可集中回读共同证据，但分别记录原因、金额、受影响项目和期间；自身 **563** 条与非自身 **79** 条分别保留来源要求。零值只能支持其明确项目，不能自动排除全部字段影响。
3. **89 组**：仅作为调度及方法分类单位。须列成员、共同事实、证据边界及例外；有不同表格、期间或采用链就继续拆分。
4. 共同事实核一次后，成员仍须完成字段、期间、取值来源及采用链检查。先修复本轮入口和故障注入，再开展批量解除。

**三个待裁定问题**

1. **引文必须含年份？**  
   不必。允许用“文档版本＋物理页＋表格标题／表号＋期间列头＋目标行列”形成可回读的绑定。仅“单位：元”或表格标题加页码不够。证据来自另一文档时，可登记来源文档与供给文档的明确关系，不能简单要求二者 ID 相同，也不能因此取消关联核验。

2. **是否新增 `MIXED_DECLARED`？**  
   不能仅凭该枚举和额外说明放行。允许两个父数来自不同发布版本，但须证明已经处于可相减的统一计量基础，必要时提供调整桥接。应核实各父的实际版本及兼容结论，不能只要求标签相等。

3. **内部枚举字段如何核证？**  
   不要求 PDF 出现内部枚举字面。应提供原文事实、实际采用记录或处理日志，以及明确的字段映射规则；例如 `period_source` 要有实际期间列和采用链，不能由封面年份映射出 `LEGACY_SNAPSHOT`。`judgement_basis` 可以解释映射，**不能替代映射所需的证据**。

本轮尚未满足批量核证入口可靠性要求，第 4、5 步继续未完成；**S04 第二轮正式验收和 S05 均维持等待。**
